- **rc-openapi**: Creates or updates an OpenAPI specification by discovering the project's HTTP endpoints, request schemas, and response models from source. Use to keep an openapi.yaml in sync after API changes, or to generate a spec for an existing service. Do not use for Postman collections (use rc-postman), README docs (use rc-readme), or editing source code. ([rodolfochicone/rc-project](https://github.com/rodolfochicone/rc-project/tree/HEAD/skills/misc/rc-openapi) · ★19)
